# 📂 File-by-File Data Provenance & Primary Source Font Mapping
This document provides 100% explicit attribution mapping for every single dataset in the repository.
> **Epistemological Distinction**:
> - **Original Primary Font / Source Authority**: The official government/institutional authority that produced and published the data (ISTAT, MIM, Eurostat, OECD, World Bank, AlmaLaurea, etc.).
> - **Open Science Data Storage / Mirror**: The repository storage host where the raw file is mirrored for zero-redirect data preservation.
